Verified: 2026-11-01

GF-G17 update: normal controls now climb Hartdown's two flights, reach the parent's
assigned bedroom, examine its actual bed and descend to the hall. Full reload
preserves both the upstairs position and a separately saved on-tread position,
including actual height. A measured 1.9 m standing proxy required an eight-foot
lower stair opening. Ground walking now excludes the neighbor's hidden tactical
duplicate wall while retaining visible walls and the planar tactical contract.
Hartdown now has continuous handrails, balusters and landing guards. Normal
controls completed the railed round trip, and another on-tread full reload
preserved XYZ. The tight turn used a camera aligned with the flights. Broader
race/equipment fit, all-angle navigation and unsupported-home admission remain open.
